Using the wonders of time compression, which he's just invented, Rich brings you warp-speed coverage of the Final here at Grand Prix Madrid. Surely nothing could top the semifinal between Reanimator and Zoo? Click here for a six minute thrill ride to close out a record-obliterating weekend. Let's do it all again very soon.

[Download MP3](https://media.ormos.uk/41/e1/41e12acd12296e8729c0956531160cae9e9b109dd06ff3f3848fd3fafaf00da4.mp3)

## Final - Andreas Müller vs. David Do Anh

*by Tobias Henke*

![](https://i.ormos.uk/42/5e/425e9fb8f9244b55d19b7004e851491175be631b3157bc41c24dc7de57e9e948.jpg)*David Do Anh prepares mentally for the finals.*

Last season the Czech Republich and Germany were the two most succesful nations on the European Grand Prix circuit. The Czech were responsible for six top-eight appearances, the Germans for 11, as well as three wins. And here we find ourselves in the final match of the first European Grand Prix 2010 with those two countries battling it out.

The matchup - Reanimator against [Ad Nauseam](https://web.archive.org/web/20211128115823/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Ad+Nauseam) Tendrils, looked likely to be an explosive one. [Ad Nauseam](https://web.archive.org/web/20211128115823/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Ad+Nauseam) Tendrils can go off fast, but Reanimator has plenty of tools to slow it down, from [Force of Will](https://web.archive.org/web/20211128115823/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Force+of+Will) and [Daze](https://web.archive.org/web/20211128115823/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Daze) to the biggest trump of all - [Iona, Shield of Emeria](https://web.archive.org/web/20211128115823/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Iona%2C+Shield+of+Emeria) .

The first game in what would be the final match started with [Brainstorm](https://web.archive.org/web/20211128115823/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Brainstorm) from Do Anh and [Thoughtseize](https://web.archive.org/web/20211128115823/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Thoughtseize) from Müller. The discard spell revealed a hand with a bunch of Rituals and [Lotus Petal](https://web.archive.org/web/20211128115823/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Lotus+Petal) s alongside [Infernal Tutor](https://web.archive.org/web/20211128115823/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Infernal+Tutor) , but not much business in the card-drawing department, so Müller took [Ponder](https://web.archive.org/web/20211128115823/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Ponder) .

On turn two... well, on turn two Do Anh went off. Two [Lotus Petal](https://web.archive.org/web/20211128115823/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Lotus+Petal) s, a fetchland, and a [Cabal Ritual](https://web.archive.org/web/20211128115823/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Cabal+Ritual) had him reach Threshold, another [Cabal Ritual](https://web.archive.org/web/20211128115823/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Cabal+Ritual) s made him reach hellbent status. [Infernal Tutor](https://web.archive.org/web/20211128115823/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Infernal+Tutor) searched [Ad Nauseam](https://web.archive.org/web/20211128115823/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Ad+Nauseam) and the black instant was cast with one black mana floating.

More [Lotus Petal](https://web.archive.org/web/20211128115823/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Lotus+Petal) s, [Cabal Ritual](https://web.archive.org/web/20211128115823/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Cabal+Ritual) , and two [Lion's Eye Diamond](https://web.archive.org/web/20211128115823/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Lion%27s+Eye+Diamond) s, [Duress](https://web.archive.org/web/20211128115823/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Duress) , and another [Infernal Tutor](https://web.archive.org/web/20211128115823/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Infernal+Tutor) followed. That of course found lethal [Tendrils of Agony](https://web.archive.org/web/20211128115823/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Tendrils+of+Agony) , or would have if not for this exchange:

“Do you want me to show you?” Do Anh asked, reaching for his library. “The Tendrils?”  
 “Nah, it's OK,” replied Müller, shrugging it off.

Andreas Müller 0 – 1 David Do Anh

![](https://i.ormos.uk/72/2b/722bd4a41e074a8c3ee62127de58db9542190922a75c6cea82aa5a7412e64c5e.jpg)*A different kind of lethal Tendrils of Agony*

The fourth turn of the match was Müller's and also it was the beginning of the second game. He cast [Careful Study](https://web.archive.org/web/20211128115823/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Careful+Study) and carefully study he did, when he drew [Iona, Shield of Emeria](https://web.archive.org/web/20211128115823/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Iona%2C+Shield+of+Emeria) and [Show and Tell](https://web.archive.org/web/20211128115823/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Show+and+Tell) . He finally chose to discard [Entomb](https://web.archive.org/web/20211128115823/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Entomb) and [Inkwell Leviathan](https://web.archive.org/web/20211128115823/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Inkwell+Leviathan) . Do Anh's first turn was not bad either with [Chrome Mox](https://web.archive.org/web/20211128115823/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Chrome+Mox) (imprinting [Mystical Tutor](https://web.archive.org/web/20211128115823/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mystical+Tutor) ), [Tropical Island](https://web.archive.org/web/20211128115823/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Tropical+Island) , and [Sensei's Divining Top](https://web.archive.org/web/20211128115823/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Sensei%27s+Divining+Top) . Only Müller had the [Force of Will](https://web.archive.org/web/20211128115823/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Force+of+Will) ready to stop the Top, though he needed to pitch his [Show and Tell](https://web.archive.org/web/20211128115823/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Show+and+Tell) to pay for it.

Next turn, Müller cast another [Careful Study](https://web.archive.org/web/20211128115823/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Careful+Study) and now without [Show and Tell](https://web.archive.org/web/20211128115823/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Show+and+Tell) he could just as well discard Iona. This was turn two and it passed on both sides of the table without further action. Turn three looked as if it might go the same way, but in Do Anh's end step Müller cast [Brainstorm](https://web.archive.org/web/20211128115823/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Brainstorm) followed by [Mystical Tutor](https://web.archive.org/web/20211128115823/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mystical+Tutor) , setting up [Thoughtseize](https://web.archive.org/web/20211128115823/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Thoughtseize) . In response to [Thoughtseize](https://web.archive.org/web/20211128115823/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Thoughtseize) it was Do Anh's turn to [Mystical Tutor](https://web.archive.org/web/20211128115823/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mystical+Tutor) . He, of course, went for the namesake card of his deck: [Ad Nauseam](https://web.archive.org/web/20211128115823/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Ad+Nauseam) , safely hiding it away on the top of his library, while [Thoughtseize](https://web.archive.org/web/20211128115823/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Thoughtseize) took [Dark Ritual](https://web.archive.org/web/20211128115823/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Dark+Ritual) out of a hand which now held one [Cabal Ritual](https://web.archive.org/web/20211128115823/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Cabal+Ritual) and one [Lotus Petal](https://web.archive.org/web/20211128115823/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Lotus+Petal) only.

In Do Anh's end step, Müller tapped out for [Brainstorm](https://web.archive.org/web/20211128115823/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Brainstorm) at which point the Czech stopped him. “ [Cabal Ritual](https://web.archive.org/web/20211128115823/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Cabal+Ritual) is a sorcery, right?” Müller asked, slightly uncertain. Turns out, it isn't. Müller smiled nervously, as he watched the powerful instant resolve. [Ad Nauseam](https://web.archive.org/web/20211128115823/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Ad+Nauseam) proceeded to draw cards amounting to 12 points of life, after which Müller was allowed to resolve his [Brainstorm](https://web.archive.org/web/20211128115823/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Brainstorm) . Do Anh discarded four lands, four [Brainstorm](https://web.archive.org/web/20211128115823/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Brainstorm) , and two [Duress](https://web.archive.org/web/20211128115823/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Duress) . You can probably imagine how good his hand was at this point.

Müller was not out of gas yet either. On his turn he brought back Iona by means of a [Reanimate](https://web.archive.org/web/20211128115823/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Reanimate) . By choosing blue, he gave Do Anh the chance to go off, but he would have to do so without card drawing or search. The only way that Do Anh had to get rid of it was with a lone [Slaughter Pact](https://web.archive.org/web/20211128115823/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Slaughter+Pact) . When Do Anh cast [Duress](https://web.archive.org/web/20211128115823/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Duress) , revealed to be holding two [Spell Pierce](https://web.archive.org/web/20211128115823/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Spell+Pierce) s, one of which went to the bin. The other was used on [Cabal Ritual](https://web.archive.org/web/20211128115823/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Cabal+Ritual) and that was good enough to at least delay Do Anh's combo for one more turn. One more turn was, of course, all it took for [Iona, Shield of Emeria](https://web.archive.org/web/20211128115823/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Iona%2C+Shield+of+Emeria) to get rid of the remaining four points of life

Andreas Müller 1 – 1 David Do Anh

Off to the final game of the final match: Do Anh started with [Sensei's Divining Top](https://web.archive.org/web/20211128115823/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Sensei%27s+Divining+Top) on turn one, Müller had [Careful Study](https://web.archive.org/web/20211128115823/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Careful+Study) , discarding Iona and a land. Do Anh used Top in his upkeep, played a fetchland and passed the turn. Likewise in his upkeep, Müller cast [Mystical Tutor](https://web.archive.org/web/20211128115823/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mystical+Tutor) , getting [Thoughtseize](https://web.archive.org/web/20211128115823/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Thoughtseize) . He cast it and once again Do Anh responded with [Mystical Tutor](https://web.archive.org/web/20211128115823/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mystical+Tutor) putting [Ad Nauseam](https://web.archive.org/web/20211128115823/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Ad+Nauseam) on top of his library.

When the [Thoughtseize](https://web.archive.org/web/20211128115823/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Thoughtseize) resolved, Müller didn't seem happy at all to see the following line-up: [Lion's Eye Diamond](https://web.archive.org/web/20211128115823/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Lion%27s+Eye+Diamond) , [Lion's Eye Diamond](https://web.archive.org/web/20211128115823/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Lion%27s+Eye+Diamond) , [Lotus Petal](https://web.archive.org/web/20211128115823/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Lotus+Petal) , and [Infernal Tutor](https://web.archive.org/web/20211128115823/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Infernal+Tutor) . No matter what he took, Do Anh would still be able to go off! To not make it too easy for him, one Diamond had to go.

In his upkeep Do Anh rearranged the top three cards of his library, so he wouldn't draw [Ad Nauseam](https://web.archive.org/web/20211128115823/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Ad+Nauseam) just yet. Instead he laid a third land, cast [Lotus Petal](https://web.archive.org/web/20211128115823/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Lotus+Petal) and [Lion's Eye Diamond](https://web.archive.org/web/20211128115823/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Lion%27s+Eye+Diamond) , sacrificing the latter, before using [Sensei's Divining Top](https://web.archive.org/web/20211128115823/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Sensei%27s+Divining+Top) to get [Ad Nauseam](https://web.archive.org/web/20211128115823/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Ad+Nauseam) .

[Daze](https://web.archive.org/web/20211128115823/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Daze) from Müller meant he had to sacrifice [Lotus Petal](https://web.archive.org/web/20211128115823/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Lotus+Petal) too, and start the combo without any mana in his pool. But with 19 life left to spend, that surely wasn't going to be a problem, was it?

![](https://i.ormos.uk/ff/2a/ff2a785433db975ab280445dd57630abc8626b6c0bd41471c0110854e6d677fe.jpg)*Andreas Muller watches on nervously as Ad Nauseam resolves*

[Sensei's Divining Top](https://web.archive.org/web/20211128115823/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Sensei%27s+Divining+Top) – 18  
[Misty Rainforest](https://web.archive.org/web/20211128115823/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Misty+Rainforest) – 18  
[Ill-Gotten Gains](https://web.archive.org/web/20211128115823/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Ill-Gotten+Gains) – 14  
[Dark Ritual](https://web.archive.org/web/20211128115823/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Dark+Ritual) – 13  
[Polluted Delta](https://web.archive.org/web/20211128115823/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Polluted+Delta) – 13  
[Mystical Tutor](https://web.archive.org/web/20211128115823/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Mystical+Tutor) – 12  
[Underground Sea](https://web.archive.org/web/20211128115823/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Underground+Sea) – 12  
[Ponder](https://web.archive.org/web/20211128115823/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Ponder) – 11  
[Orim's Chant](https://web.archive.org/web/20211128115823/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Orim%27s+Chant) – 10  
[Dark Ritual](https://web.archive.org/web/20211128115823/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Dark+Ritual) – 9  
[Orim's Chant](https://web.archive.org/web/20211128115823/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Orim%27s+Chant) – 8  
[Cabal Ritual](https://web.archive.org/web/20211128115823/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Cabal+Ritual) – 6  
[Infernal Tutor](https://web.archive.org/web/20211128115823/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Infernal+Tutor) – 4

Still, he had not found a single source of mana, that didn't require an input of mana first. Without any in his pool, Do Anh was forced to press on. He turned the next card: [Tendrils of Agony](https://web.archive.org/web/20211128115823/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Card/Details.aspx?name=Tendrils+of+Agony) ! Down to zero! We have a winner! As his deck's kill spell turned on him, Do Anh slumped back in his chair. Müller raised his hands to his head in exultation and disbelief, as the crowd erupted in applause.

Congratulations to Andreas Müller, the champion of the largest Magic tournament in history!
